Extra proofs (2 theorems from expanded bank)

Proof 1: Noether's theorem: every continuous symmetry gives a conserved quantity

Statement: Noether's theorem: every continuous symmetry gives a conserved quantity

Hypotheses: Lagrangian L(q,q_dot,t), continuous symmetry

Conclusion: conserved charge Q

Definitions: Noether, symmetry, conservation

Proof:

Step 1: Invariance under translation => momentum conservation

Step 2: Invariance under rotation => angular momentum conservation

Step 3: Invariance under time translation => energy conservation

Step 4: General: Noether charge from symmetry generator

ID: d6a86b394b68 | Status: UNFORMALIZED | Complexity: O(n)

Mutation test: One of the most profound results in physics

Provenance: Domain Mechanics, theorem #1

Proof 2: Hamilton's equations: q_dot = dH/dp, p_dot = -dH/dq

Statement: Hamilton's equations: q_dot = dH/dp, p_dot = -dH/dq

Hypotheses: H(q,p,t) Hamiltonian

Conclusion: equations of motion in phase space

Definitions: Hamilton, canonical, phase space

Proof:

Step 1: Legendre transform: H = p*q_dot - L

Step 2: dL = (dL/dq)dq + (dL/dq_dot)dq_dot

Step 3: Substitute Euler-Lagrange

Step 4: Rearrange into Hamilton form

ID: 3382549af063 | Status: UNFORMALIZED | Complexity: O(n^2)

Mutation test: Equivalent to Lagrangian mechanics, more symmetric

Provenance: Domain Mechanics, theorem #2
